
FUNCTION 'SDT 70056 - Employee Lost Time' GLOBAL
    IF (WebAutomation.IfWebPageContains.WebPageContainsElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... eeLostTime\'']['Div \'/70056 - Employee Lost Time\'']) THEN
        LOOP FOREACH CurrentDepertmentStage IN Depertment_SDT_70056
            SET SubflowRetryCount TO 0
            SET MaxSubflowRetries TO 2
            SET DownloadSuccess TO $'''False'''
            LOOP WHILE SubflowRetryCount < MaxSubflowRetries AND DownloadSuccess = $'''False'''
                WebAutomation.GoToWebPage.GoToWebPage BrowserInstance: Browser Url: Data70056 WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... eeLostTime\'']['Div \'Shift General\'']) FOR 30
                ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    GOTO 'Retry Dept 70056'
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    GOTO 'Retry Dept 70056'
                END
                @@RepairWithAIVision: 'True'
WebAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextFieldUsePhysicalKeyboard BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... eeLostTime\'']['Input text \'combobox\' 2'] Text: Shift Mode: WebAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace UnfocusAfterPopulate: True WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                ON ERROR REPEAT 10 TIMES WAIT 3
                END
                WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... eeLostTime\'']['Div \'Date *\'']) FOR 30
                ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    GOTO 'Retry Dept 70056'
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    GOTO 'Retry Dept 70056'
                END
                @@RepairWithAIVision: 'True'
WebAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextFieldUsePhysicalKeyboard BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... eeLostTime\'']['Input text \'dtpDate_70056emplost\''] Text: $'''%FormattedDateTime% - %FormattedDateTime%''' Mode: WebAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace UnfocusAfterPopulate: True WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                ON ERROR REPEAT 10 TIMES WAIT 3
                END
                WAIT (WebAutomation.WaitForWebPageContent.WebPageToContainElement BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... eeLostTime\'']['Div \'[7A] Sewing-RIBDepartment *\'']) FOR 30
                ON ERROR WaitForWebPageContentError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    GOTO 'Retry Dept 70056'
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    GOTO 'Retry Dept 70056'
                END
                @@RepairWithAIVision: 'True'
WebAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextFieldUsePhysicalKeyboard BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... eeLostTime\'']['Input text \'combobox\''] Text: CurrentDepertmentStage Mode: WebAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace UnfocusAfterPopulate: True WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60
                ON ERROR REPEAT 10 TIMES WAIT 3
                END
                LABEL 'Retry Download for SDT 70056'
                WAIT 2
                WebAutomation.PressButton.PressButtonPressDialogButton BrowserInstance: Browser Control: appmask['Web Page \'h ... eeLostTime\'']['Button \'DOWNLOAD EXCEL\''] WaitForPageToLoadTimeout: 60 DialogButton: $'''OK'''
                ON ERROR PressButtonError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    GOTO 'Retry Dept 70056'
                END
                WAIT (UIAutomation.WaitForWindowContent.WindowToContainElement Element: appmask['Window \'70056 - Employee Lost Time - Google Chrome\'']['Window \'Save As\'']) FOR 300 
                ON ERROR WaitForWindowContentError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    GOTO 'Retry Dept 70056'
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    GOTO 'Retry Dept 70056'
                END
                UIAutomation.PopulateTextField.PopulateTextField TextField: appmask['Window \'70056 - Employee Lost Time - Google Chrome\'']['Edit \'File name:\''] Text: $'''%Download_Path%\\%CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-Employee Lost Timer for 70056 (%CurrentDepertmentStage%).xls''' Mode: UIAutomation.PopulateTextMode.Replace ClickType: UIAutomation.PopulateMouseClickType.SingleClick
                UIAutomation.PressButton Button: appmask['Window \'70056 - Employee Lost Time - Google Chrome\'']['Button \'Save\''] Simulate: False
                ON ERROR PressButtonError
                    SET SubflowRetryCount TO SubflowRetryCount + 1
                    GOTO 'Retry Dept 70056'
                END
                WAIT (File.WaitForFile.Created File: $'''%Download_Path%\\%CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-Employee Lost Timer for 70056 (%CurrentDepertmentStage%).xls''') FOR 120 
                ON ERROR TimeoutError
                    GOTO 'Retry Download for SDT 70056'
                END
                SET DownloadSuccess TO $'''True'''
                LABEL 'Retry Dept 70056'
                IF DownloadSuccess = $'''False''' THEN
                    WAIT 3
                END
            END
            IF (File.IfFile.DoesNotExist File: $'''%Download_Path%\\%CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-Employee Lost Timer for 70056 (%CurrentDepertmentStage%).xls''') THEN
                Variables.AddItemToList Item: $'''❌ %CurrentFactory%-%FormattedDateTimeForNaming%-Employee Lost Timer for 70056 (%CurrentDepertmentStage%) - Failed after %MaxSubflowRetries% attempts''' List: Missing_Files
            END
        END
    ELSE
        Variables.AddItemToList Item: $'''⚠️ %CurrentFactory% - SDT 70056 - Report page not available''' List: Missing_Files
    END
END FUNCTION
